\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 17. VOICE-SYN Decision\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\fs24 \cf0 VOICE-SYN complete with explicit limitations; ready for VOICE-AUDIT.
\f1\b0 \
Rationale: the completed B1\'96B4 extraction set gives a strong enough evidence base for audit. B1 establishes admissions and programme task realities; B2 supplies the descriptor spine; B3 supplies professional/process and self-tape evidence; B4 closes the key safety and fairness gap around no-diagnosis and access/non-deficit handling.\
There are no critical blockers. No source row remains unresolved. Several rows are partial, duplicate, out of scope, provenance-limited or not found, and those limitations must be preserved in VOICE-AUDIT.\
Live-output examples are not required before VOICE-AUDIT, because the audit will assess baseline/rubric gaps against the source synthesis. Live Song / Voice outputs remain required later for output-specificity/live QA.\
Recommended mode for VOICE-AUDIT: 
\f0\b standard ChatGPT with file uploads enabled
\f1\b0 , not Deep Research. No new public-web extraction is needed unless the audit discovers a material missing handoff.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 18. Reusable Handoff Pack for VOICE-AUDIT\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\fs28 \cf0 VOICE-SYN handoff summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 VOICE-SYN is complete with explicit limitations and ready for VOICE-AUDIT. The Voice / Singing evidence base now covers VOICE-S001\'96VOICE-S052 across official admissions/programme sources, formal exam frameworks, professional/casting/self-tape process sources, and accessibility/fairness/vocal-health-safety sources. Every source row is closed with a final status or explicit limitation; no source row remains unresolved.\
The strongest synthesis result is that Song is not technique-only display. Across admissions, formal frameworks and professional sources, sung work is consistently both vocal and interpretive: pitch, rhythm, tone, diction, phrasing and technical control sit alongside lyric intention, communication, character, story, style and acting-through-song. This supports clarifying 
\f2\fs26 vocal
\f1\fs24  as sung-vocal evidence in Song and Musical Theatre while keeping acting/storytelling visible as a supporting category and avoiding acting-scene leakage.\
The formal descriptor spine is now strong: pitch/intonation, rhythm/time, tone, diction/intelligibility, phrasing/shape, communication and interpretation are well supported. Style evidence is useful but uneven. Classical/legit, contemporary MT, classical art song, unaccompanied and accompanied/backing-track contexts are stronger; belt, mix, jazz, folk and broad commercial/pop descriptors remain thinner and must be carried as limitations.\
B1\'96B3 strongly support assessability-first self-tape handling: clear audio, balanced accompaniment, readable framing, simple capture and workable room acoustics are conditions for fair assessment, not vocal merit. B4 strengthens this with resource-equity evidence: devices, data, assistive technology, adaptive equipment and home environment affect participation and assessability, not talent.\
B4 also closes the major safety risk: vocal-health language must be no-diagnosis and observation-only. Strain-like sound, fatigue, hoarseness or reduced range may be described cautiously but never diagnosed or treated as pathology. Accessibility evidence supports non-deficit handling of disability, neurodivergence, hearing difference, communication support, accent, gender-diverse voice and reasonable adjustments.\
Live-output behaviour remains untested: Song/Voice labels, timestamp rendering, score stability and report specificity must be handled later in output-spec/live QA.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Source-family evidence summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 B1 admissions sources strongly support song contrast, task-aware repertoire, acting-through-song, accompaniment differences, self-tape assessability and live-room boundaries. B2 formal frameworks strongly support descriptor language but must not supply TapeCoach weights. B3 professional/casting/self-tape sources strongly support assessability versus polish, lyric/storytelling, accompaniment handling and process-only callbacks. B4 safety/access sources strongly support no-diagnosis, non-deficit access handling, accent fairness, hearing access, gender-diverse voice caution and resource-equity guardrails.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Style/subtype evidence summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Strongest: classical/legit, contemporary MT, classical art song/opera-adjacent, unaccompanied singing, accompanied/backing-track song, audition cuts, MT song in multi-component tape. Medium: actor-musician, self-accompanied song, commercial/pop vocal. Thin: belt, mix, detailed registration, jazz, folk and broader commercial-pop descriptors.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Tape-observable versus process-only summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Finished song tape can usually support pitch, timing, diction, phrasing, tone, lyric intention, acting-through-song, visible communication, accompaniment balance and basic assessability if audio/video conditions allow. It cannot safely prove response to direction, learning speed, sight-singing, aural skills, callback readiness, training potential, stamina, health status, access need, identity, or professional readiness beyond observable task compliance and assessability.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Safety/accessibility/fairness summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Use no-diagnosis language. Treat strain-like or fatigued sound as cautious observation only. Do not infer pathology, respiratory condition, convalescence, psychological state or medical status. Treat hearing access, captions, STT, sign interpretation, assistive tech, reasonable adjustments, disability, neurodivergence, accent, speech difference and gender-diverse voice as access/fairness context, not performance deficits.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Generic-feedback risk summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 High-risk generic phrases include \'93strong vocal control\'94, \'93lovely tone\'94, \'93good breath support\'94, \'93secure pitch\'94, \'93emotional connection\'94, \'93powerful belt\'94, \'93nice mix\'94, \'93good legit quality\'94, \'93expressive singing\'94, \'93great musicality\'94, \'93strong storytelling\'94, \'93professional song choice\'94, \'93perfect song choice\'94, \'93vocal health sounds good\'94, \'93ready because of voice type\'94, \'93strong presence\'94 and \'93clean audio as vocal merit\'94. These require observable anchors or must be blocked.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Preliminary risk-resolution summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 VOICE-PRE-I03 is resolved as a safety boundary. VOICE-PRE-I09 and VOICE-PRE-I11 are strongly reduced. VOICE-PRE-I01, I02, I05, I06, I07, I08, I10 and I12 are partially resolved and must be audited. VOICE-PRE-I04 and I13 remain largely output/display/live-QA risks.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Limitation register summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Carry forward: thin belt/mix/registration detail; uneven pop-rock/jazz/folk/commercial descriptors; partial actor-musician evidence; B3 singer-specific process partly opera/generic shaped; S034 snippet limitation; S037 opinion/paywall limitation; S041 opera/remote-context limitation; S050 accent/hiring focus; S051 broad digital/equipment access; S052 not found; live outputs absent; display labels unverified; timestamp rendering and score stability untested; no codified score anchor tables; no separate musical-interpretation field.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Audit priority preview\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 VOICE-AUDIT should prioritise: vocal technique versus story balance; sung-vocal field semantics; style/subtype specificity; song-only acting/storytelling without acting-scene leakage; musical interpretation inside existing fields; assessability versus polish; audio/technical versus vocal merit; no-diagnosis vocal-health boundary; accessibility/inclusive voice protections; accent/diction fairness; gender-diverse voice caution; repertoire/fixed-material boundary; live-room overclaim; generic praise; timestamp density; MT non-regression; Acting and Dance label non-regression.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Baseline preservation notes\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 No finding implies a score-field, weighting, cap, blocker, verdict, role-fit, schema, backend, pipeline or server-side recomputation change. The current live production architecture remains preserved. MT acting + song remains the protected regression anchor.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Exact source ID range used\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 VOICE-S001\'96VOICE-S052, with shorthand S### normalised to VOICE-S###.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 What was and was not synthesised\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Synthesised: B1\'96B4 source evidence, source-family boundaries, descriptor spine, safety/fairness evidence, claim-scope limits, baseline compatibility and audit priorities.\
Not synthesised as verified product behaviour: live Song / Voice report outputs, frontend label behaviour, comparison-page behaviour, rendered timestamp parity, score stability, implementation behaviour or production rollout effects.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 19. Completion Statement\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 VOICE-SYN complete with explicit limitations. Ready for VOICE-AUDIT.\
}
